| note | INSTANCE | 400×534 | 💡 Learnings · Factorial - Cancel dataset sync in progress When we are syncing data sometimes there is an error that the dataset is stuck in fetching information or another step in the process. We need to ask data/integrations to force it to error which means that we don't have any autonomy as a user. We should be able to have a button that is cancel so that we can try again. | 2 / 1 / 0 | [open](https://www.figma.com/design/rg5IG7leRJXbj8HP93Jnsk/?node-id=2320-181485) |
| note | INSTANCE | 400×534 | 💡 Learnings · Ability to cancel an Excel datasync When uploading an Excel, we may upload a file and realize that there is a formatting error (incorrect column order or date format). However, there's no way to cancel the sync process. Instead, we have to wait for the sync to lead to an error or have Engineering unblock the issue for us. | 2 / 1 / 0 | [open](https://www.figma.com/design/rg5IG7leRJXbj8HP93Jnsk/?node-id=2321-181505) |
